codex
**Verdict: FAIL. G1 is not ready to pass, including with narrowing.** The package establishes usable environmental contrasts, but it has not established an identifiable transfer endpoint, independent validation cohort, or adequate preprocessing provenance. These are substantive gaps.

I read the requested records and handoff §§5–7. All 12 manifested local files match their recorded sizes and SHA-256 hashes. Broad CSV checks reproduce the matrix dimensions, pairing counts, and reported direction correlations. I made no edits and inspected no additional Sanger outcome values. Current Figshare manifests and the S3 Zenodo archive were inaccessible through the available routes, so their contents remain independently unverified.

1. **[CRITICAL] The named question is not yet an operational, answerable estimand.**  
   [g1_question_candidate.md:4](/Users/bmarti44/github/bufferright/research/cancer-dependency-rsi/reviews/g1_question_candidate.md:4) leaves “transfer degradation of dependency behavior” undefined. A shift in dependency levels, loss of expression–dependency association, and loss of predictive rank correlation are different outcomes. The required genome-wide development matrices are still unavailable.

   **Fix:** Name one numerical transfer endpoint, its reference population, comparator, eligible targets, and required inputs. Demonstrate that those inputs can actually be joined. Published NextGen-versus-traditional test tables cannot replace individual-level prediction data while retaining the same question; that fallback is a different descriptive study.

2. **[CRITICAL] Outcome-guided panel selection creates a circularity risk that acknowledgment does not resolve.**  
   [provenance_v1.md:7](/Users/bmarti44/github/bufferright/research/cancer-dependency-rsi/data/provenance_v1.md:7) acknowledges selection using Broad 2D–3D dependency differences. The proposed transfer endpoint may reuse those differences or their constituent observations. Conditioning on lineage and baseline strength does not automatically remove this selection effect.

   **Fix:** Trace the exact selection inputs and their overlap with every proposed evaluation cohort. Treat Broad reuse as development evidence. External confirmation can support a claim conditional on the fixed selected panel only after excluding donor overlap and outcome-dependent shared processing. Do not generalize to unselected genes.

3. **[CRITICAL] Preprocessing independence remains unaudited.**  
   [provenance_v1.md:27](/Users/bmarti44/github/bufferright/research/cancer-dependency-rsi/data/provenance_v1.md:27) records a flag rather than a resolved dependency graph. Choosing a study-specific release does not establish independence. Sanger’s public pipeline also contains transformations fitted using outcomes across samples, including spline correction and ComBat. This establishes an audit requirement, not proof that A and C are contaminated. [Sanger processing code](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Garnett-Lab/SangerOrganoidBiobank/main/scripts/CRISPR_pipeline.R).

   **Fix:** Record which observations fitted each correction, library parameter, normalization, and selection rule. Establish whether any evaluation outcomes influenced development artifacts. Where they did, reconstruct development-only processing or obtain independent evidence. Reserve “transductive covariate preprocessing” for covariate-only operations.

4. **[HIGH] Seven models is the overall count, not the independent count for either contrast.**  
   The pair keys in [identifiability_audit.json:14](/Users/bmarti44/github/bufferright/research/cancer-dependency-rsi/data/identifiability_audit.json:14) imply:

   | Contrast | Matched contrasts | Distinct models | Verified independent donors |
   |---|---:|---:|---|
   | Medium | 5 | **4** | Unresolved |
   | Format | 7 | **6** | Unresolved |

   ACH-003040 contributes twice to each contrast type and is the only complete four-condition model. Treating all contrasts equally also gives it extra weight.

   **Fix:** Report contrast-specific model and donor counts; define weights and retain shared-model covariance. Genes are correlated targets, not additional patients. A joint bootstrap preserves dependence only if its grouping and estimand are correct; it does not create additional biological support.

5. **[HIGH] “Heterogeneity dominates” is unsupported by the reported sign-flip statistic.**  
   [identifiability_audit.json:53](/Users/bmarti44/github/bufferright/research/cancer-dependency-rsi/data/identifiability_audit.json:53) turns any positive and negative observed changes into evidence of biological heterogeneity. My Broad-only check found:

   | Gene group | Medium: both signs | Format: both signs |
   |---|---:|---:|
   | 250 test genes | 90.8% | 98.4% |
   | 750 controls | **92.1%** | **98.1%** |

   For illustration, independent symmetric zero-effect noise produces both signs with probability \(1-2^{1-k}\): 93.75% for five observations and 98.44% for seven. That is not a calibrated null for this dependent design, but it demonstrates why sign flips alone prove little.

   **Fix:** Retract “heterogeneity dominates” and “average effects are near-uninterpretable.” Assess magnitudes against technical variation and controls. Report meaningful opposing effects where supported. Do not expand into poorly supported context strata because tiny estimates cross zero.

6. **[HIGH] The required observation-level provenance and attrition registry is missing.**  
   [provenance_v1.md:26](/Users/bmarti44/github/bufferright/research/cancer-dependency-rsi/data/provenance_v1.md:26) leaves donor crosswalks open. The record does not supply the handoff’s complete model/donor/origin/laboratory/library/condition/replicate table or attempted-condition attrition.

   The Broad README documents 34 sequencing attempts and a removed replicate; the analysis matrix contains collapsed screen estimates. Zero matrix missingness does not establish complete experimental coverage.

   **Fix:** Build the observation table and distinguish unattempted conditions, growth failures, QC exclusions, and unavailable metadata. Recover replicate identities and document which uncertainty components are estimable. Resolve ACH-003052 or exclude it from claims requiring the missing annotation.

7. **[HIGH] Treatment scope and confounding need explicit limits.**  
   The interventions change bundles: plastic versus Matrigel dome changes matrix exposure as well as geometry; OPAC versus RPMI/FBS changes medium composition beyond serum. Sparse condition coverage also limits separation of model, lineage, origin, and interaction effects.

   **Fix:** Name the actual treatment bundles. Restrict intervention interpretations to observed within-model comparisons. Do not claim isolated geometry, serum, genotype modification, or causal explanation of external transfer failure. Across-cohort association remains compatible with laboratory, library, state, and population differences.

8. **[HIGH] The Sanger validation outcome is not established by the downloaded candidate file.**  
   [provenance_v1.md:33](/Users/bmarti44/github/bufferright/research/cancer-dependency-rsi/data/provenance_v1.md:33) reports 930 ACH columns, whereas the covariates describe HCM-SANG organoids. A crosswalk cannot substitute for establishing whether the required organoid measurements are present. The continuous LFC matrix is named but absent from the supplied manifest.

   **Fix:** Establish object identities, cohort membership, screen/library identifiers, target coverage, and the authoritative continuous-outcome route through schema and documentation. Resolve duplicate-library selection. Binary BAGEL calls require a different endpoint and cannot silently replace continuous rank-transfer evidence. Ranking avoids absolute-scale equivalence; it does not eliminate assay effects.

9. **[HIGH] The Sanger glimpse is disclosed, but its disposition is insufficient.**  
   [exposure_log.jsonl:2](/Users/bmarti44/github/bufferright/research/cancer-dependency-rsi/data/exposure_log.jsonl:2) labels 18 cells “de minimis” without identifying their targets, samples, or relationship to eligible C observations. The 930-column schema makes even the description “Sanger glimpse” uncertain.

   **Fix:** Recover the displayed identifiers from retained transcripts without reopening values; map them to candidate cohorts and document downstream decisions. At minimum, affected claims lose an entirely outcome-unseen designation. Conservatively exclude exposed donors or targets where appropriate, or use fresh evidence if influence cannot be bounded. **The entire Sanger resource need not automatically be discarded, but current handling does not justify an intact blinding claim.**

10. **[HIGH] Layer B cannot become untouched merely through a later split.**  
    [partitions_provisional.json:8](/Users/bmarti44/github/bufferright/research/cancer-dependency-rsi/data/partitions_provisional.json:8) proposes carving B from Layer A after whole-panel outcome inspection and aggregate analyses.

    **Fix:** Reserve genuinely uninspected evidence or explicitly classify later evaluation on these observations as reused-data engineering evaluation. Gene or pathway partitions within the same measured models do not establish independent biological tasks. Final manifests must record exposure history alongside membership.

11. **[HIGH] A plausible novelty gap remains, but novelty has not been established at the proposed resolution.**  
    [novelty_audit_v1.md:13](/Users/bmarti44/github/bufferright/research/cancer-dependency-rsi/reviews/novelty_audit_v1.md:13) overstates “nobody has tested.” S1 reports individual-gene environmental findings and paired results, not only class summaries. S2 includes context-specific functional findings; S3 already examines transfer attenuation and distinguishes target-wise prediction from within-organoid ranking. [S1](https://www.nature.com/articles/s41586-026-10843-7), [S2](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC13581617/), [S3](https://www.mdpi.com/2079-7737/15/19/1782).

    **Fix:** Complete the listed supplement/code reads and preserve exact searches and exclusions locally. Audit each precise claim against prior figures, targets, and endpoints. The potentially open contribution is a bounded relationship between measured environmental contrasts and independently evaluated transfer degradation—not heterogeneity alone or familiar lipid/adhesion effects. Failure of S3 to answer that question does not establish that this design can answer it.

12. **[HIGH] Falsification and precision rules remain inadequate.**  
    [g1_question_candidate.md:7](/Users/bmarti44/github/bufferright/research/cancer-dependency-rsi/reviews/g1_question_candidate.md:7) offers several movable alternatives. “No association” is not a falsifier when intervals are wide. A positive association can coexist with an effect below δ. Choosing δ from attainable precision risks redefining scientific importance to fit available data.

    **Fix:** Define one primary parameter, a scientifically justified practical threshold, disjoint decision rules, and an inconclusive region. Perform a precision-feasibility assessment using four/six environmental model clusters and the actual eligible transfer donors. Seven models do not automatically prohibit every panel-wide association; they do not justify an adaptive gene-class × genotype × context search either.

13. **[MEDIUM] Manifest completeness and reproducibility fall short of “verified provenance.”**  
    [source_manifest_sanger_figshare.json:1](/Users/bmarti44/github/bufferright/research/cancer-dependency-rsi/data/source_manifest_sanger_figshare.json:1) omits the downloaded Jaccard and VAF files. Neither manifest pins an article version or preserves the authoritative manifest response. Local hash agreement establishes file integrity, not source accuracy. Also, [audit_minipool.py:59](/Users/bmarti44/github/bufferright/research/cancer-dependency-rsi/src/audit_minipool.py:59) does not regenerate the JSON’s direction-check results.

    **Fix:** Manifest every imported source, pin versions/commits, retain source responses and license evidence, and make the audit regenerate its reported outputs. Document the primary-source QC discrepancy: Methods state 500,000 reads, while Extended Data Fig. 9 states five million.

The required narrowing is substantial: **one fixed environmental contrast, one fixed eligible target family, and one panel-wide associational transfer parameter**, conditional on the selected genes and measured models. For example, medium contrasts in dome have four model supports. Remove adaptive context-stratification and causal-attribution promises unless additional evidence supplies identification. Published group-comparison tables could support a separate descriptive concordance question, which needs its own novelty justification.

Before G1 can pass, complete the crosswalk/count/attrition table; resolve preprocessing independence and the actual transfer-data route; correct the heterogeneity interpretation; adjudicate exposure and B reuse; and document an exact, plausibly novel, feasible question. Phase 2 can then finalize the endpoint, meaningful margins, baselines, uncertainty procedure, multiplicity, eligibility, partitions, invalid-result rules, and hashes required for Lock 1. Those later implementation requirements should not be confused with the unresolved G1 evidence requirements.

S2 includes context-specific functional findings; S3 already examines transfer attenuation and distinguishes target-wise prediction from within-organoid ranking. [S1](https://www.nature.com/articles/s41586-026-10843-7), [S2](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC13581617/), [S3](https://www.mdpi.com/2079-7737/15/19/1782).

    **Fix:** Complete the listed supplement/code reads and preserve exact searches and exclusions locally. Audit each precise claim against prior figures, targets, and endpoints. The potentially open contribution is a bounded relationship between measured environmental contrasts and independently evaluated transfer degradation—not heterogeneity alone or familiar lipid/adhesion effects. Failure of S3 to answer that question does not establish that this design can answer it.

12. **[HIGH] Falsification and precision rules remain inadequate.**  
    [g1_question_candidate.md:7](/Users/bmarti44/github/bufferright/research/cancer-dependency-rsi/reviews/g1_question_candidate.md:7) offers several movable alternatives. “No association” is not a falsifier when intervals are wide. A positive association can coexist with an effect below δ. Choosing δ from attainable precision risks redefining scientific importance to fit available data.

    **Fix:** Define one primary parameter, a scientifically justified practical threshold, disjoint decision rules, and an inconclusive region. Perform a precision-feasibility assessment using four/six environmental model clusters and the actual eligible transfer donors. Seven models do not automatically prohibit every panel-wide association; they do not justify an adaptive gene-class × genotype × context search either.

13. **[MEDIUM] Manifest completeness and reproducibility fall short of “verified provenance.”**  
    [source_manifest_sanger_figshare.json:1](/Users/bmarti44/github/bufferright/research/cancer-dependency-rsi/data/source_manifest_sanger_figshare.json:1) omits the downloaded Jaccard and VAF files. Neither manifest pins an article version or preserves the authoritative manifest response. Local hash agreement establishes file integrity, not source accuracy. Also, [audit_minipool.py:59](/Users/bmarti44/github/bufferright/research/cancer-dependency-rsi/src/audit_minipool.py:59) does not regenerate the JSON’s direction-check results.

    **Fix:** Manifest every imported source, pin versions/commits, retain source responses and license evidence, and make the audit regenerate its reported outputs. Document the primary-source QC discrepancy: Methods state 500,000 reads, while Extended Data Fig. 9 states five million.

The required narrowing is substantial: **one fixed environmental contrast, one fixed eligible target family, and one panel-wide associational transfer parameter**, conditional on the selected genes and measured models. For example, medium contrasts in dome have four model supports. Remove adaptive context-stratification and causal-attribution promises unless additional evidence supplies identification. Published group-comparison tables could support a separate descriptive concordance question, which needs its own novelty justification.

Before G1 can pass, complete the crosswalk/count/attrition table; resolve preprocessing independence and the actual transfer-data route; correct the heterogeneity interpretation; adjudicate exposure and B reuse; and document an exact, plausibly novel, feasible question. Phase 2 can then finalize the endpoint, meaningful margins, baselines, uncertainty procedure, multiplicity, eligibility, partitions, invalid-result rules, and hashes required for Lock 1. Those later implementation requirements should not be confused with the unresolved G1 evidence requirements.

**Recommend one fixed, panel-wide association using the six-model format contrast and independently evaluated molecular-prediction loss.** It is potentially G1-passable after resolving data access and preprocessing provenance. **Nothing currently establishes a G1 pass**, and an imprecise null would not rescue the gate.

The [review’s three critical failures](/Users/bmarti44/github/bufferright/research/reviews/g1_review_r1_verdict.md:1) require an operational endpoint, separation from selection outcomes, and an audited processing dependency graph.

1. **Preferred: format lability versus external prediction loss.** Retains the scientific connection between controlled environmental measurements and molecular transfer. Requires individual-level conventional-model data, Sanger continuous outcomes and molecular covariates, and the provenance audit described below.
2. **Matrix-saving alternative: transfer of a fixed 2D consensus target ordering.** Use `AdherentMean` as the frozen source ranking and evaluate its ordering errors across genes within Sanger organoids. This avoids fitting conventional-model predictors. It is a different question: consensus target prioritization, with no claim about loss of genotype/expression relationships or improvement beyond S3’s molecular baseline.
3. **Processing fallback: independent public measurements or pre-joint-correction processed outputs.** Retain the first route’s estimand but replace evidence whose processing crosses the development/evaluation boundary. This remains conditional on locating suitable public observations; “reprocess independently” is not a concrete solution when required inputs are unavailable.

**1. One primary estimand for the preferred route**

Choose **format**, based on its six model supports. The intervention bundle is **plastic culture versus Matrigel-dome culture, holding the recorded medium fixed**. It does not isolate geometry from matrix exposure.

For gene \(g\), define measured format lability:

\[
s_g=\frac{1}{6}\sum_{m=1}^{6}
       \frac{1}{|K_m|}\sum_{k\in K_m}
       \left|Y_{mgk,\mathrm{Dome}}-Y_{mgk,\mathrm{Plastic}}\right|.
\]

Here \(K_m\) contains the observed matched media. ACH-003040’s two medium-specific contrasts receive half its model weight each; every other model contributes one contrast. Each model therefore receives weight \(1/6\).

The target family is the **224 test genes outside the exact 200-gene selection tail**, subsequently restricted only by frozen identifier, assay-coverage and source-estimability rules. This is one selected family, with equal target weights.

For transfer:

- Train a molecular dependency predictor \(f_g\) using conventional models only.
- Reserve conventional reference donors \(R\) disjoint from training donors \(T\).
- Apply the **same fitted predictor** to \(R\) and donor-disjoint Sanger organoids \(C\).
- Evaluate within colorectal, oesophageal, gastric, ovarian and pancreatic groups, with equal prespecified group weights. Required source coverage must be demonstrated before locking these groups.
- Retain one sample/library per donor using an outcome-independent selection hierarchy.

For domain \(D\in\{R,C\}\), define target-specific donor-ordering accuracy:

\[
C_g^D=\frac15\sum_{\ell=1}^{5}
 P\!\left[
 (f_g(X_i)-f_g(X_j))(Y_{ig}-Y_{jg})>0
 \mid i,j\text{ distinct donors in group }\ell
 \right],
\]

with ties receiving half credit. Then define transfer loss:

\[
d_g=C_g^R-C_g^C.
\]

Positive values mean worse ordering in organoids. This compares ordering accuracy without subtracting Chronos from Sanger LFC.

The **single primary parameter** is the finite-panel linear-projection coefficient

\[
\boxed{\displaystyle
\beta=
\frac{a^\top M_Xd}{a^\top M_Xa},
\qquad a_g=s_g/\operatorname{IQR}(s).
}
\]

\(M_X\) removes the fixed baseline covariates: source dependency mean and SD, source ordering accuracy, signed and absolute Broad selection-table `MeanDifference`, and the library’s functional-class indicators. Use their fixed column space; overlapping classes do not justify adaptive variable selection.

**Interpretation:** additional loss of correct donor ordering per one-IQR increase in measured format lability, conditional on those baselines, this target family and these environmental supports.

The predictor comparison must include lineage-only, genotype-only, S3-style expression Elastic Net, and genotype-plus-expression models, using the same permitted observations. Select the predictor family and tune it entirely within \(T\), then freeze it before evaluating \(R\) and \(C\). Environmental scores enter the explanatory association, not external predictor selection.

This route still needs the individual-level source matrix. Published group means cannot supply \(C_g^R\).

**2. Selection circularity: exact exclusion plus conditional external evaluation**

The downloaded trace supports this reproducible rule:

1. In `organoids_vs_2d_gene_dependency_tests_full.csv`, retain `DropEssential=False`.
2. Sort by signed `MeanDifference`.
3. Take the lowest 100 and highest 100 genes. **Do not substitute an FDR cutoff.**
4. Match the table’s `SYMBOL (EntrezID)` identifiers to library genes.
5. Exclude their intersection with `All test genes=True`.

That removes **26 of 250 test genes**, leaving **224** before other eligibility exclusions. The tail boundaries have no ties.

However, **224 does not mean selection-independent genes**. Whole functional classes were chosen using the same comparison. The [library documentation](/Users/bmarti44/github/bufferright/research/cancer-dependency-rsi/data/raw/broad_figshare/README.txt:1) and enrichment trace make that pathway-level selection explicit.

The remaining protection is therefore:

- Condition the claim on the entire historical panel-selection process and fixed family.
- Use the Broad comparison table only as development information and frozen baseline covariates.
- Obtain the evaluation outcome from Sanger donors excluded from all overlapping Broad environmental, selection, training and reference cohorts.
- Exclude uncertain donor matches from confirmatory eligibility.
- Establish that Sanger evaluation outcomes did not influence any source predictor, selection artifact or environmental-score processing artifact.

This can neutralize **reuse of the selection outcome as confirmation**. It cannot identify a genome-wide, selection-unconditional association. Adjusting for `MeanDifference` alone would not establish independence.

**3. Independent units and achievable precision**

Format supplies **seven contrasts across six models**, at most six independent environmental donors pending crosswalk completion. Medium supplies five contrasts across four models; restricting medium to dome gives four clean supports.

The 224 genes are correlated targets. They are not 224 environmental experiments.

There are two different inferential ambitions:

- **Conditional on the six measured environmental supports:** a panel-wide association is feasible in principle. External donors provide repeated evaluations of a fixed vector of measured lability scores. Cross-target dependence must be retained by resampling complete donor outcome vectors.
- **For latent biological sensitivity or new environmental models:** technical measurement error and environmental-model sampling matter. Neither is resolved by having many genes. With collapsed screen estimates and unaudited replicate uncertainty, the latent-sensitivity coefficient remains unidentified.

A rough precision check illustrates the limitation. If a model-cluster influence SD were \(s_u\), an approximate 95% half-width would be:

\[
t_{.975,5}s_u/\sqrt6\approx1.05s_u
\]

for format, versus \(1.59s_u\) for four-model medium. An influence SD of 0.10 already produces approximately ±0.105 for format, before other uncertainty. These are feasibility illustrations, not calibrated intervals for deliberately selected models.

For external ordering accuracy, under a simple independent-donor null, its SE is approximately \(1/(3\sqrt N)\): about 0.027 at 150 donors. A difference between two equally sized domains has SE around 0.038 per target under those assumptions. Donor pairs are dependent; their quadratic count does not provide quadratic precision.

A shared coefficient can be more precise than individual target losses, but that depends on the **actual joint covariance**, residual lability variation after conditioning, and environmental-score uncertainty.

**Verdict:** a conditional panel-wide parameter is plausible. “Only a bound” is not forced by \(n=6\), and a useful bound is not guaranteed. No actual precision claim is defensible before the eligible donor counts and joint uncertainty are established. A bound on *true biological sensitivity* additionally needs a defensible measurement-error model.

**4. Sanger’s permitted role**

Sanger can provide external evidence for the frozen association after establishing continuous-outcome identities, target coverage, donor crosswalks, library selection and processing provenance. Do not inherit S3’s cohort count as this study’s eligible count.

Its pipeline fits spline corrections using cross-sample LFC means and applies ComBat across libraries. Those operations use outcomes and can alter ranks. If fitted wholly within the external cohort, they do not automatically contaminate Broad training, but they couple external observations and require processing-aware uncertainty or a clearly conditional processed-cohort interpretation. If evaluation outcomes influenced development artifacts, the affected processing must be reconstructed or replaced. [Sanger processing code](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Garnett-Lab/SangerOrganoidBiobank/main/scripts/CRISPR_pipeline.R).

The quarantined BAGEL matrix cannot supply this continuous ordering endpoint. Its 930-column schema also does not establish the intended organoid cohort.

Recover the 18 displayed cell identifiers from retained transcripts without reopening values. Map them to donors and targets. Conservatively exclude implicated external donors, record target exposure, and assess whether that exposure influenced family or endpoint choices. Unrecoverable exposure prevents an intact outcome-unseen claim; it does not automatically invalidate the entire resource.

Sanger cannot identify a causal culture contribution to transfer loss. Platform, library, population and processing differences remain alternative explanations.

**5. Falsification, inconclusive and invalid-result rules**

Propose **\(\delta=0.10\) ordering-accuracy loss per lability IQR**.

Its justification is experimental prioritization: ten additional incorrect orderings per 100 donor comparisons materially degrade a ranking tool; a drop from 0.60 accuracy to 0.50 removes its useful discrimination. This is a proposed decision-based margin, not an established clinical threshold. Its utility rationale must be accepted before evaluation and cannot be enlarged to match attainable precision.

Using one prespecified joint 95% interval \([L,U]\):

| Result | Decision |
|---|---|
| \(L>0.10\) | Support the practically meaningful positive association. |
| \(U<0.10\) | Falsify the claim of an association at least this large. |
| Entire interval lies inside \((-0.10,0.10)\) | Support practical equivalence for this conditional parameter. |
| \(U<-0.10\) | Support a practically meaningful association in the opposite direction. |
| \(L\le0.10\le U\) | Inconclusive about the primary meaningful-positive claim. |

A small positive estimate can therefore falsify the meaningful-positive claim. A nonsignificant estimate with a wide interval cannot.

Additional rules:

- Unresolved provenance, donor identity, score direction or joins: **invalid for confirmation**.
- No demonstrated above-chance source prediction: **no established source relationship whose transfer can be assessed**.
- Zero residual lability variation after baseline conditioning: parameter undefined.
- Material dependence on one environmental model or plausible measurement-error assumptions: no robust biological interpretation; report that limitation.
- One primary coefficient receives the confirmatory decision. Target-specific, pathway and medium analyses remain exploratory.

A bound here concerns the usefulness of the **measured lability score** for explaining this transfer endpoint. It does not bound the total causal contribution of culture conditions.

**6. Novelty sentence**

*The candidate contribution is a selection-conditioned estimate, or informative bound, of the association between measured plastic-to-Matrigel-dome dependency lability and independently evaluated loss of within-lineage donor-ordering accuracy from frozen molecular dependency predictors.*

That distinguishes the estimand from S1’s environmental findings, S2’s dependency/biomarker maps and S3’s transfer evaluation. It remains a candidate novelty claim until the outstanding supplement/code audit checks this exact relationship.

Taking this route requires recording the following limits against the ambitious goal:

- Drop causal attribution to culture, isolated geometry/serum effects, and genotype × state × context discovery.
- Drop genome-wide and new-environmental-model generalization.
- Drop intrinsic heterogeneity and invariance claims unsupported by replicate-level uncertainty.
- Retain an atlas of all 250 test genes, explicitly marking the 26 primary-family exclusions and every subsequent exclusion.
- Keep **C6 unmet** until an externally supported finding or sufficiently precise bound actually passes.
- Keep **C8/C9 unmet**: this narrowed study does not establish policy improvement or 20% resource savings, and exposed Layer A cannot become untouched Layer B through a later split.
- Record that a G1 design approval would not complete the broader explanatory goal or the RSI objective.

No files were edited, and no additional Sanger outcome values were inspected.

Cancer dependency research with an autonomous RSI loop

Goal and executable handoff for a local research agent

Prepared 28 October 2026. Treat every source below as a seed to verify against the current primary source, release manifest, and paper methods.


1 - The ambitious goal

Build and run a laptop-based autonomous research system that answers a specific, still-open question about when molecularly defined cancer dependencies persist or fail across measured culture environments.

The primary question is whether measured sensitivity to culture conditions, together with genotype and tumor state, can explain failures of dependency transfer from conventional cancer models to patient-derived organoids beyond lineage and technical variation alone.

Produce all of the following.

- At least one previously unreported, precisely bounded, externally supported explanatory finding. It may be a positive conditional dependency rule, a convincing refutation of a meaningful dependency claim, or an informative bound that materially changes how a target should be interpreted. It must resolve a specific uncertainty, not merely increase a benchmark score.
- A reproducible evidence atlas covering every eligible gene in the controlled environmental panel, including support, contradiction, heterogeneity, uncertainty, missing evidence, and excluded genes with reasons. Expansion beyond that selected panel is exploratory unless independently justified.
- A defensible connection between measured environmental sensitivity and transfer behavior in an independently filtered organoid cohort, where the available evidence supports that connection.
- An implemented research-policy improvement loop with real experiments, faithful historical replay, executable policy revisions, fresh deployment, and a final comparison against strong fixed exploration strategies.
- Evidence that the learned policy produces a practically meaningful improvement under matched total resources, including its own learning overhead.
- A manuscript and complete reproducibility package ready for independent expert review, with all findings, failures, limitations, and costs disclosed.

Seek a finding that changes which cancer target evidence researchers should trust. Do not substitute a generic prediction leaderboard or an agent framework demonstration for that scientific objective.

Ambition does not authorize changing the standard of evidence. If the scientific result or the policy improvement fails its criteria, the overall goal remains unmet. A valid negative result can qualify only when it resolves a predeclared meaningful uncertainty with sufficient precision. An insignificant p-value does not qualify.


2 - Your operating role

You are the lead investigator, research engineer, statistician, and experiment coordinator for this project.

Execute the work. Do not stop after writing a plan, creating folders, or describing an architecture. Use the current session to establish the scientific contract, build the smallest working vertical slice, evaluate real public observations, and begin the first actual policy-improvement cycle when the evidence and resources permit.

Use frontier model reasoning through the capabilities already supplied by your host agent. All scientific fitting, simulation, evaluation, and data processing must run on this laptop. Public web access and public data services are allowed. No new wet-lab measurements, controlled-access patient data, paid scientific cloud compute, or local foundation-model training are required.

Make routine research and implementation decisions autonomously. Ask only when a missing credential, explicit spending authority, unavailable required file, or genuinely consequential unresolved user choice prevents further authorized work. Complete all independent work first.

Work inside an isolated project directory named cancer-dependency-rsi under the current workspace, or continue the existing project if its identity is unambiguous. Preserve unrelated files and existing instructions.

Install dependencies in a project environment. Respect existing permissions and approval controls. Do not copy permission-bypass flags from upstream agent launch scripts. Do not submit papers, publish repositories, contact researchers, purchase services, or provision new infrastructure.

Use specialist workers when available, with distinct roles for literature and novelty, data provenance, scientific modeling, evaluator implementation, and adversarial review. The evaluator and final data access must have a narrower interface than the proposing agent. Shared privileged access is not a genuine security boundary and must never be described as one.


3 - Mandatory starting evidence

Verify and read the methods, data availability, supplements, and closest relevant findings before treating any seed as an established fact.

S1 - Broad NextGen resource and environmental experiments

https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC13581591/
https://doi.org/10.1038/s41586-026-10843-7
https://github.com/broadinstitute/DepMap-NextGen-Public
https://doi.org/10.6084/m9.figshare.29472362

S2 - Sanger organoid resource

https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC13581617/
https://doi.org/10.1038/s41586-026-10830-y
https://github.com/Garnett-Lab/SangerOrganoidBiobank
https://doi.org/10.6084/m9.figshare.28339340
https://cellmodelpassports.sanger.ac.uk/downloads
https://dataminer.depmap.sanger.ac.uk/
https://depmap.sanger.ac.uk/documentation/datasets/wg-crispr-knockout/

S3 - Closest direct transferability prior

https://doi.org/10.3390/biology15191782
https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42883455/
https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC13664187/
https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.22472622

S4 - Current Broad data and release guidance

https://depmap.org/portal/data_page/?tab=allData
https://forum.depmap.org/t/organoid-dependency-data/4671
https://forum.depmap.org/t/announcing-the-26q3-release/4692

S5 - Dream-RSI method and implementation

https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14858
https://arxiv.org/html/2609.14858v2
https://github.com/google-research/dream-rsi

The starting literature establishes several constraints.

- The Broad resource reports 147 genome-scale screens, while the Sanger resource reports screens across 162 organoids. These counts are not automatically disjoint biological samples.
- The smaller controlled Broad environmental experiment has 250 deliberately selected test genes and 18 screens across only seven biological models. It includes seven matched format contrasts and five matched medium contrasts, with possible repeated models. Verify the exact design in the source.
- The 250 genes were selected partly because they differed between model environments. They are not a representative genome-wide sample.
- The October 2026 transferability study already examines expression-based transfer from conventional models to Broad and Sanger organoids for 20 selected genes. Generic 2D-to-organoid transfer is not the new contribution.
- Broad's temporary NextGen manuscript dataset was incorporated into DepMap Public 26Q3. Staff direct users to the integrated CRISPRGeneEffect.csv and relevant model metadata. Reverify the current route rather than inventing a file ID or assuming old instructions still work.
- Sanger processed fitness measurements and Broad Chronos gene effects are different measurement systems. Their absolute values are not interchangeable.
- Historical releases, a current integrated release, and independently produced laboratory data are different forms of evidence. Reprocessing the same observations is not biological replication.

Resolve downloads through current official manifests. Record source URL, release or commit, retrieval date, license, byte size, and checksum. Do not invent direct download URLs or accession metadata. Use official alternative routes if a public portal blocks unattended retrieval. Do not bypass a challenge or access control.

Build around public processed observations. Raw sequencing is unnecessary. If a proposed analysis requires paired observations that are not actually released, identify that limitation before implementing the model.


4 - Hardware, budget, and persistence

Inspect the available RAM, CPU, disk, operating system, installed runtimes, and already configured model access without printing secrets.

Set conservative defaults and save them in a machine-readable configuration.

- No GPU dependency.
- At most two CPU-heavy jobs concurrently.
- An aggregate research memory ceiling of the smaller of 8 GiB and 60 percent of physical RAM, reduced further when necessary to leave the host responsive.
- A ten-minute default timeout for an individual exploratory fitting job. Longer jobs require an explicit recorded allocation from the existing campaign budget.
- A download ceiling of 20 GiB or one quarter of available disk space, whichever is smaller.
- A first pilot target of 24 real evaluated development nodes and three executable policy challengers, retaining the incumbent.
- An eight-hour ceiling for the first pilot invocation. This is a safety ceiling, not a promised completion time.
- No automatic increase in financial, disk, concurrency, or wall-time ceilings.

Use the host agent's already authorized model access. Any additional programmatic metered API calls require a numeric user-configured cap or an existing explicitly authorized cap. If none exists, the additional API spending cap is zero. Continue with host-driven reasoning and local computation where possible, and build the adapter without dispatching unapproved calls.

Do not require the user to buy new services just to start the project. Do not silently equate an unknown price with zero.

For separately dispatched API calls, enforce limits in code. Reserve a conservative maximum cost before each request, count concurrent reservations and retries, reconcile against returned usage, and persist the ledger before dispatch. Do not call a provider whose billable maximum cannot be bounded by the configured limits.

Record model identifiers, prompts, token counts when exposed, tool costs, wall time, CPU time, and peak memory. If host-model costs cannot be measured, mark them unknown. You may still do science, but you cannot certify an all-in cost improvement while material costs are unobserved.

Use append-only event records and checkpoint after every completed experiment. Track proposed, dispatched, completed, failed, and committed states. An interrupted request remains reserved until reconciled. Never reset the budget or repeat a completed paid request on resume.

Do not claim a process is running unless you have an actual live process or job identifier and recent heartbeat. If the host session ends, write the exact next action and resume command. Do not promise work will continue after the runtime stops.


5 - Data and identifiability audit

Before adaptive hypothesis search, construct a provenance table with biological model, donor, tumor lineage, sample origin, laboratory, assay library, medium, growth format, replicate identity, release, and measured outcome.

Create explicit crosswalks among Broad, HCMI, Sanger, and author-specific identifiers. Mark uncertain donor identity as uncertain rather than assuming independence.

Determine which observations are truly paired. Count independent biological models and donors. Do not count genes, guide RNAs, wells, alternative cultures, or technical replicates as additional independent patients.

Audit release composition and preprocessing provenance. An integrated release may contain validation observations or corrections learned jointly from them. Removing overlapping rows alone does not undo leakage through outcome-guided target selection, normalization, or model selection. Prefer compatible study-specific releases and transformations fitted on development data. If a fitted correction used external dependency outcomes, rebuild a development-only analysis or obtain genuinely independent validation evidence. Disclosure alone does not restore independence. Distinguish that outcome leakage from covariate-only joint preprocessing, which must be disclosed as transductive feature preprocessing with appropriately limited claims. This is a required provenance check, not an assertion that any named release is contaminated.

Verify dependency score direction, dynamic range, controls, missingness, and quality flags separately for each source. Avoid direct subtraction of Chronos values from Sanger fold-change or essentiality scores.

Preserve the seven-model environmental design as a small, selected experiment. Determine whether row-level paired outcomes, replicates, and required metadata are sufficient for the intended contrast. A batch correction cannot separate laboratory and biology when the experimental design confounds them.

Record attrition explicitly, including models unable to grow in a condition and genes excluded for insufficient variation or precision.

Data eligibility is determined from prespecified quality and identifiability rules. It must not be selected later to maximize a favorable result.

The gate is an identifiable, falsifiable question in a named subset. If the data cannot support the original question, propose and document a narrower still-open question within the same scientific objective. Do not pretend the original broader goal has been achieved.


6 - Scientific estimands and research contract

Start from two connected estimands.

The direct environmental estimand is the change in a gene's measured dependency between environment A and environment B within the same biological model, while holding the other measured culture condition fixed. Analyze medium and growth format separately where the design permits.

The transfer estimand is the extent to which a prespecified genotype, tumor-state, or context relationship predicts dependency behavior in an independently filtered organoid cohort beyond lineage and the strongest appropriate baseline.

Use original documented assay units for paired within-study contrasts. Use justified ranking, calibration, or outcome-specific comparisons for cross-study evidence.

For each environmental claim, report the dependency in both conditions, all individual paired changes, uncertainty, and heterogeneity. A near-zero average can conceal large opposing effects.

Do not call a dependency invariant because a difference is nonsignificant. If claiming equivalence, define a scientifically justified equivalence margin before confirmatory evaluation and require the relevant interval to fall inside that margin. If no defensible margin exists, report continuous effects and uncertainty without an invariance label.

A stable absence of dependency is not a stable cancer vulnerability. Establish that the dependency itself is meaningful in the environments covered by a positive claim.

Do not interpret a predictive expression feature as a causal mediator without identifying assumptions and suitable evidence. Environmental intervention evidence and cross-cohort biomarker associations must retain distinct evidence labels.

Carry uncertainty and shared-model dependence in estimated environmental sensitivity into downstream transfer analyses using a justified measurement-error, resampling, or sensitivity analysis. Do not treat noisy estimated sensitivities as exact independent inputs. Report conclusions that change materially across plausible environmental-effect estimates.

During initial development, choose and justify the precise primary endpoint, practical effect margin, hypothesis family, baseline set, uncertainty procedure, and sensitivity analyses. Then freeze them before policy optimization.

The research contract must include the following.

- Exact question and falsifiable alternatives.
- Intended population, environments, targets, and generalization claim.
- Eligibility and exclusion rules.
- Data and identifier versions.
- Development, policy-evaluation, and final external partitions.
- Feature engineering and transformation rules.
- Primary and secondary endpoints with fixed direction.
- Minimum practical effects or equivalence margins and their justification.
- Multiplicity control and uncertainty methods.
- Baseline definitions.
- Invalid-result rules.
- Success, failure, inconclusive, and budget-exhaustion conditions.

The initial lock fixes the permitted hypothesis and transformation spaces, partitions, evaluator, baseline definitions, practical margins, and inferential rules. Adaptive development may select hypotheses, features, hyperparameters, and fitted artifacts within those spaces. A second lock commits the exact selected claim family, predictions, and artifacts before external evaluation.

Hash this contract, evaluator code, and split manifests. Changing a locked scientific contract creates a new study identifier and requires appropriate new evaluation evidence.


7 - Novelty and biological scope

Maintain a claim ledger throughout the project.

For each proposed claim, record the nearest prior result, which part is already established, the precise remaining uncertainty, evidence needed to resolve it, and the consequence if the claim is supported or refuted.

Read the Broad environmental analyses, the Sanger biomarker analyses, and the October transfer study closely. Their reported lipid, adhesion, state, and dependency findings are controls or prior evidence. Recovering them is not a new discovery.

Use current primary literature, author data, and official documentation. Date the search and preserve queries and important exclusions. Absence of an exact search hit is not proof that a claim is new.

Choose a bounded hypothesis family before final evaluation. Consider gene classes with measured environmental sensitivity and adequate overlap, not an unconstrained scan of every feature until a small p-value appears.

Before finalizing the manuscript, repeat the novelty audit for each surviving exact claim. Preserve contradictions and prior evidence. Do not silently rename a known association to make it appear new.

The required scientific contribution must be more specific than weak organoid transfer, better average correlation, or successful agent automation.


8 - Baselines, inference, and adversarial checks

Build simple competent baselines before allowing elaborate models.

Before the final lock, reproduce baselines only on development data or independent control data. Any reproduction requiring final external outcomes must occur after the final hypotheses, predictions, artifacts, and inferential code are committed, alongside the final evaluation. Those results cannot inform revisions to the tested study.

Where applicable compare lineage only, genotype only, tumor-state or expression only, genotype plus state, measured context or assay covariates, and a regularized linear predictor modeled on the existing transfer study.

Use the same eligible observations and permitted information for each comparison. Learn preprocessing, feature selection, dimension reduction, and tuning within development folds. Do not optimize a feature representation on final external outcomes.

Compare against the strongest applicable baseline, not only the weakest. If environmental information is central to the explanation, include a prespecified ablation removing it.

Use effect sizes and calibrated predictive or uncertainty endpoints as the scientific evaluator's optimization target. Do not reward p-value reduction, the number of discoveries, manuscript length, or confidence of the LLM explanation.

Treat adaptive development as exploratory. Evaluate a locked final claim family once on independent outcomes. For a small confirmatory family, Holm correction at familywise alpha 0.05 is a reasonable default. Any alternative must be justified and frozen before outcomes are revealed.

Do not apply ordinary fixed-family correction to an indefinitely adaptive stream of repeatedly inspected p-values and call it confirmatory evidence.

Use uncertainty at the donor or biological-model level, accounting for repeated cultures and target or pathway dependence where relevant. Many bootstrap repetitions do not create new biological replication. With few clusters, assess whether the proposed interval or test is justified and report the effective sample size.

Perform relevant negative controls, leakage checks, donor and lineage sensitivity analyses, platform sensitivity, and leave-one-model-out checks. Permutations must respect exchangeability and grouping. Do not permute labels freely when the design makes that invalid.

Synthetic data may test code, calibration, and identifiability under explicit assumptions. Synthetic labels, imputed outcomes, and LLM guesses cannot serve as independent biological validation.

Only make claims supported by the actual evidence. Public dependency screens alone do not establish clinical efficacy, normal-tissue safety, treatment resistance reversal, or universal target validity.


9 - Three distinct evaluation layers

Keep these layers separate.

A - Development task panels

Use them to build scientific artifacts, tune development models, generate discovery trees, and select policy challengers. Record every adaptive use.

B - Reserved policy evaluation panels

Use them to compare the final learned exploration policy with fixed strategies in fresh rollouts. Freeze the compared policies and selection criterion before these panels are evaluated.

C - Final external biological evaluation

Prefer an independently filtered Sanger cohort after resolving donor overlap, release provenance, assay compatibility, and adequate representation. Use final outcomes only after committing the hypotheses, predictions, model artifacts, and inferential code.

A final external cohort cannot also be a repeatedly consulted policy reward. Do not send final Sanger outcomes into the replay pool.

For each final claim, identify exactly which component each dataset validates. External confirmation of dependency transfer does not independently validate a medium or format effect, or establish that environmental sensitivity caused transfer failure. Retain adaptively reused environmental evidence as exploratory or selection-adjusted evidence, as justified. Do not manufacture an independent environmental test by splitting genes from the same seven models. Seek an additional existing public controlled-environment dataset when the selected claim requires independent environmental confirmation. If none is suitable, narrow the claim or retain the unmet evidence gate.

Subdividing the selected 250 genes does not create hundreds of independent biological tasks. Record model, donor, gene, and pathway overlap in all panels, and use blocked evaluation. The larger development cohorts may provide engineering tasks for policy learning, but their transfer to the small environmental problem must be reported honestly.

Within a single privileged local agent, enforce a logged protocol with a separate evaluator interface and restricted inputs. Do not claim cryptographic secrecy or genuine blindness merely because an outcome file lives in a separate directory.

Use retrospective external validation as the default wording. Public literature and model pretraining can expose outcomes. Record known exposure and do not certify that public results were unknowable.

A failed final test is a failed final test. Subsequent work that uses its outcomes becomes exploratory unless genuinely new independent evidence is obtained.


10 - Implement the actual RSI loop

Keep the base scientific generator, its model version and prompting contract, data contract, and evaluator fixed during a policy comparison.

Make the editable policy an executable module that receives observed history, legal actions, and remaining resources, and selects an action.

Legal actions may include opening a predefined analysis family, choosing an existing branch, refining a particular artifact, running a permitted diagnostic, and stopping.

The policy may alter branching, prioritization, exploration versus refinement, and stopping. It cannot change labels, splits, metrics, practical margins, multiplicity correction, or success criteria.

A narrative reflection is not a policy improvement unless it changes executed decisions.

Record each discovery node's immutable parent, exact artifact, input context hash, data and split hashes, evaluator hash, seeds, result, validity diagnostics, failures, model calls, and measured costs.

Historical replay may reveal only previously executed artifacts and their actual recorded outcomes. It must not use a model to invent outcomes for unexplored analyses.

A reordered branch may alter the context seen by a generator. Make refinement depend on an immutable parent and fixed context, or require an exact context match before replaying a recorded child. Do not assume a recorded child represents an arbitrary counterfactual generation.

The replay policy sees only the outcomes revealed along its trajectory. Do not expose the full tree's future rewards.

Mark unsupported replay actions unsupported. Do not assign them fake scores or treat a surrogate prediction as a measured outcome. Compare policies on common support, report support coverage, and use replay as a screening tool.

Perform the following cycle.

1. Run the initial fixed policy on real development tasks.
2. Preserve the evaluated discovery trees as replay worlds.
3. Generate at least three executable policy challengers plus the unchanged incumbent.
4. Replay them under the same resource constraints and support rules.
5. Select a challenger with the frozen selection rule.
6. Test the challenger in fresh development rollouts against fixed exploration using the same generator and evaluator.
7. Promote only when the preregistered criterion is met.
8. Retain the incumbent when challengers fail.
9. Add only development histories and repeat.

Complete at least two genuine cycles before the final policy comparison if the authorized resources and valid tasks permit. Two cycles do not require two promotions. An unchanged incumbent is a valid experimental outcome.

After development, freeze the best admissible policy and evaluate it on the reserved policy panels. Compare it with the original fixed exploration strategy and at least one simple fixed-allocation or random-selection strategy.


11 - What counts as demonstrated policy improvement

Predeclare one primary operating point or cost-to-quality endpoint. Also report complete scientific-quality-versus-cost curves.

Use the same generator, evaluator, initial artifacts, information permissions, and common resource ceilings for all policies.

Charge policy rewriting, replay, failed jobs, promotion trials, and extra model calls to the improving system. Permit fixed baselines to spend the corresponding budget on scientific search.

Report both end-to-end performance including learning overhead and deployment-only performance with learning costs shown separately.

The ambitious engineering target is at least 20 percent lower total measured API expenditure to reach a predeclared valid scientific-quality threshold, under the same CPU ceiling and information constraints, compared with the strongest fixed baseline. This is a project target, not a forecast.

Set the quality threshold from development evidence before final policy evaluation. It must be meaningful and attainable by competent baselines, not chosen after observing which policy wins.

Use paired blocked comparisons where appropriate and report uncertainty. For the default 20 percent savings goal, require the prespecified one-sided 95 percent lower confidence bound on savings to meet the 20 percent practical margin. A point estimate above 20 percent with a lower bound merely above zero supports some improvement but does not meet that ambitious margin. Freeze how failures to reach the quality threshold within budget are treated as censored outcomes. Do not convert nonattainment into invented exact costs. Random seeds quantify execution variability, not independent biological replication.

If API expenditure is not the dominant or measurable resource, choose a justified alternative primary resource endpoint during the initial contract stage, explain it, and lock it before optimization. All other material resources must still be measured or strictly matched. A CPU-only advantage or token proxy cannot satisfy C9 while material host-model costs remain unknown and unmatched. In that case retain an unresolved cost gate and avoid claiming all-in savings. Define an equally explicit practical improvement margin and uncertainty requirement for any alternative endpoint. Do not switch endpoints after seeing final results.

If faithful replay has inadequate support, preserve that finding and use fresh rollouts to assess the proposed policy. Label the method accurately as a Dream-RSI-inspired adaptation and disclose departures.

Do not call the system recursively self-improving merely because more compute improved a reused validation score.


12 - Completion criteria

Mark the ambitious goal complete only when every applicable criterion below has documentary evidence.

C1 - A current novelty audit identifies a precise unresolved cancer dependency question and distinguishes it from the August resources and October transfer study.

C2 - All analyses use legitimately accessible public data, with complete provenance, licensing, identifier crosswalks, and reproducible retrieval or documented local cache installation.

C3 - The intended paired contrast or conditional relationship is identifiable within a named population. Confounding and selection are disclosed, and independent biological units are counted correctly.

C4 - The scientific contract, eligibility rules, splits, baseline set, practical effects, evaluator, and confirmatory procedures were frozen before adaptive optimization and final evaluation.

C5 - The pipeline reproduces relevant published or author-code baseline results closely enough to establish outcome direction, cohort integrity, and measurement interpretation, with discrepancies resolved or explicitly bounded.

C6 - At least one previously unreported explanatory finding passes the frozen scientific criteria and relevant external tests. A supported refutation or informative bound may qualify when it resolves a meaningful predeclared uncertainty. Mere nonsignificance, a rediscovered association, or a better exploratory score does not qualify.

C7 - The evidence atlas covers all eligible genes in the controlled panel and lists every exclusion. Every entry distinguishes dependency strength, environmental change, uncertainty, heterogeneity, biomarker evidence, transfer evidence, and prior knowledge.

C8 - At least two real policy-improvement cycles have run, with executable challengers, faithful replay, fresh rollout evaluation, retained failed attempts, and a final frozen-policy comparison on reserved panels.

C9 - The learned policy meets the preregistered practical improvement criterion against the strongest fixed baseline under matched total resources. Meeting only a deployment-only criterion does not satisfy an end-to-end claim.

C10 - Scientific conclusions survive the prespecified multiplicity correction, appropriate uncertainty analysis, key ablations, and adversarial checks. Any claim of invariance requires positive dependency evidence and justified equivalence evidence.

C11 - A clean reproduction run regenerates the primary estimates, figures, evidence decisions, and policy comparison within documented tolerances from frozen artifacts. It must not require rerunning stochastic LLM search to reproduce the reported analysis.

C12 - The package includes a manuscript for expert review, machine-readable evidence, source and data manifests, all experiment histories, model and policy versions, actual resource measurements, failures, limitations, and exact reproduce and resume commands.

C13 - The first meaningful end-to-end research cycle has actually executed. A plan, scaffold, mock evaluator, or hypothetical example is insufficient.

Seek multiple convergent findings across adequately represented lineages and pathways as an additional scientific stretch target. Do not replace one strong finding with a quota of weak associations. Do not force claims about sparsely represented cancer types.

Keep scientific discovery, prediction improvement, and policy improvement as separate status fields. One does not establish the others.


13 - Required artifacts and commands

Create a compact repository with equivalent artifacts to the following. Choose actual file formats suited to the content.

- README.md with scope, installation, exact commands, and current limitations.
- GOAL.md with the locked goal and completion criteria.
- PROTOCOL.md and a machine-readable locked research contract.
- STATUS.md and a structured status record with current phase, incumbent, spending, next action, and unmet gates.
- A data manifest, source manifest, donor/model crosswalk, eligibility registry, and split manifests.
- A novelty and claim ledger with the complete decision history.
- Data adapters, baseline models, a fixed evaluator, policy modules, a faithful replay engine, a local runner, and a budget wrapper.
- An append-only experiment ledger and immutable discovery artifacts.
- Frozen final predictions, scientific models, policies, and checksums.
- An evidence atlas in a machine-readable table.
- Figures, a manuscript draft, a limitations statement, and an adversarial-review report.
- Environment and dependency locks, licenses and attribution, and a reproducibility report.
- Meaningful tests for identity leakage, score direction, grouping, estimator behavior, cost accounting, resume idempotence, unsupported replay actions, and final-outcome access.

Provide simple documented commands equivalent to bootstrap, audit-data, reproduce-baselines, run-pilot, run-cycle, evaluate-policy, lock-science, evaluate-external, reproduce, status, and resume.

Do not overbuild a general agent platform before the first valid scientific vertical slice works. Reuse maintained components where that reduces risk, but inspect their resource demands and permissions.


14 - Terminal outcomes and reporting

Use explicit terminal states.

- COMPLETE_SUPPORTED_DISCOVERY
- COMPLETE_SUPPORTED_REFUTATION_OR_BOUND
- SCIENTIFIC_RESULT_WITHOUT_RSI_EVIDENCE
- RSI_RESULT_WITHOUT_SCIENTIFIC_DISCOVERY
- INCONCLUSIVE
- BLOCKED
- BUDGET_EXHAUSTED

The first two require every relevant completion gate, including demonstrated policy improvement. The others are honest outcomes and must list which parts of the ambitious goal remain unmet.

A completed computational campaign is not automatically a completed scientific goal. Do not relabel insufficient power as equivalence or a polished manuscript as a discovery.

When blocked or budget-limited, checkpoint all valid work, identify the exact missing condition, and provide the smallest concrete next step. Do not lower scientific standards, spend beyond the cap, or retry indefinitely.

During execution, report concise milestones with measured results, unresolved risks, and the next discriminating experiment. Avoid progress theater based on file counts or unexecuted plans.


15 - Start now

Inspect the workspace and resource limits. Verify the primary sources and accessible processed data. Build the donor and assay provenance audit. Formulate a narrowly identifiable first question and its strongest alternative explanations.

Implement the smallest valid data-to-estimate-to-evaluator path. Reproduce the necessary baseline. Freeze the initial research contract and evaluation partitions.

Then run real development experiments, build the first replay world, generate executable policy challengers, and perform a fresh comparison. Continue through the authorized staged campaign.

Your first substantive report must show what was actually executed, what the observations establish, what remains unidentifiable, which policy was retained or promoted, the measured costs, and the exact next action. If a real evidence or authorization gate prevents the first cycle, report that precise blocker and all completed independent work. Do not claim that the loop started when only its scaffolding exists.

# Provenance & identifiability audit v2 (2026-10-28) — supersedes v1 (G1 review 56/100 remediation)

All v1 factual errors corrected below are independently re-verified from primary files. Review: `reviews/g1_review_r1_verdict.md`.

## Environmental panel (Broad minipool) — corrected counts
- 18 screens in released Chronos matrix (18×1000, 0 missing) from **34 sequencing attempts** (35 rows in sequence metadata incl. pDNA reference); 16/18 screens attempted with technical duplicates; **1 replicate removed** (`0178_Adh_Nat_Serum_Dome_RepB`, low NNMD) — screen retained on remaining replicate. Observation-level table: `data/observation_provenance_minipool.csv` (35 rows: model, lineage where known, lab=Broad, library, medium, format, replicate, reads, in-matrix flag, attrition notes).
- **Contrast-specific independent support (corrected):** medium bundle (OPAC vs RPMI/FBS) = 5 pairs / **4 distinct models**; format bundle (Matrigel dome vs plastic) = 7 pairs / **6 distinct models**. `ACH-003040` contributes 2 pairs to each (only complete 4-condition model) — equal-pair weighting overweights it; Lock 1 defines model-level weighting. **Donor counts UNRESOLVED** for all 7 models (no donor field in released metadata); models ≠ verified independent donors.
- Condition coverage per model is sparse (2–4 of 4 cells); released metadata does **not** distinguish unattempted vs growth-failed vs QC-excluded for missing cells → recorded as "not in released matrix; reason unavailable". Zero matrix missingness ≠ complete experimental coverage.
- **Treatment bundles (named honestly):** medium = full OPAC↔RPMI/FBS composition change (not isolated serum); format = plastic↔Matrigel-dome (geometry + matrix exposure, not isolated geometry). Interpretations restricted to observed within-model bundle contrasts.
- **QC discrepancy in primary source (documented, unresolved):** S1 Methods state ≥500,000 reads across replicates; Results/Fig. 5 text states ≥5×10⁶ total readcounts + NNMD ≤ −1.5. Observed screen reads (6.7M–59M) satisfy both; no analysis impact identified, recorded for fidelity.
- Lineages (6/7): ACH-000048 ovarian endometrioid, ACH-000108 pancreas adenosquamous, ACH-000178 pancreatic adenocarcinoma, ACH-001418 serous ovarian, ACH-002958 stomach adenocarcinoma (organoid), ACH-003040 pancreatic adenocarcinoma (organoid). **ACH-003052 (organoid) lineage absent from all released Broad metadata** → excluded from any lineage-conditioned analysis until resolved; format contrasts (within-model) unaffected.

## Statistics — retraction
v1's "heterogeneity dominates / averages near-uninterpretable" is **retracted**. Sign-flip rates for test genes (medium 90.8%, format 98.4%) match 750 controls (92.1%, 98.1%) and zero-effect noise expectations. Magnitude evidence: median |Δ| medium 0.183 (test) vs 0.164 (controls); mean |mean Δ| 0.137 vs 0.094 — modestly larger test-gene effects, consistent with selection, not proof of heterogeneity. All numbers regenerable via `src/audit_minipool.py` → `data/identifiability_audit.json` (audit v2).

## Selection trace & circularity control
- Library (250 genes) designed from functional classes + the genome-wide 3D-vs-2D comparison: "200 outlier genes" = top 100 each tail of mean dependency difference excluding common essentials (S1 Fig. 5), enriched gene-set clusters then informed classes.
- **Tier-A exclusion (independently reproduced):** 26/250 panel genes are direct 200-outlier members → excluded from primary analyses (`data/selection_exclusions.json`), leaving 224; 1 further attrition (`TMOD` absent from transfer table) → **223 joined genes**.
- Residual pathway/class-level selection cannot be excluded per-gene. **All claims are conditional on the fixed selected panel; no generalization to unselected genes.** Broad-side associations are development/descriptive evidence only.

## Preprocessing dependency graphs (audited from source text/code)
- **Broad genome-wide Chronos:** all NextGen screens used Humagne library → library batch **confounded** with organoid-ness; authors pre-trained Chronos on traditional + NextGen **CNS** models, then applied parameters to organoids. Consequence: NextGen Chronos values (incl. the organoids-vs-2D table, n=94 vs 433) are not outcome-independent of NextGen data; usable as **development** evidence, never as independent validation of Broad-derived selections. Minipool Chronos computed "in the same manner" on enAsCas12a screens (separate library); within-model paired contrasts share processing within pairs.
- **Sanger:** pipeline = CRISPRcleanR (per-sample CN correction) → spline normalization (expression bias) → **ComBat batch correction across samples** → BAGEL2 binary calls (verified in `Garnett-Lab/SangerOrganoidBiobank/scripts/CRISPR_pipeline.R`). ComBat/spline parameters are fitted across the cohort's outcomes → processed Sanger matrices are **not** development-independent by construction. Authoritative processed route per S2: Cell Model Passports + DepMap Miner; figshare holds figure-reproduction files incl. `07_EssMatrix_qnorm_corrected_logFCs.RData` (continuous LFCs; **not downloaded**) and BAGEL binary depletions (downloaded, quarantined; 17,647×930 ACH — a DepMap-wide binary matrix, **not** itself the 162-organoid continuous outcome). Confirmatory use requires, at Lock 1/2: cohort identity via crosswalk, duplicate-library rule (2 libraries/model in places), and either development-only refit from counts (`sgRNAs-counts…csv` exists on figshare) or explicit downgrade of independence claims. Binary BAGEL ≠ continuous rank endpoint; never substituted silently.
- Assay scales (Chronos vs corrected LFC) remain non-interchangeable; cross-study = rank/calibration only.

## Exposure adjudication (final)
Glimpsed cells (from retained transcript, file not reopened): genes `A1BG, A1CF, A2M` × models `ACH-001421, ACH-001412, ACH-000888, ACH-001184, ACH-000317, ACH-000264`. All 6 models are **traditional 2D cell lines** (Broad metadata, `IsNextGen=False`); none of the 3 genes is in the 250 panel. **Zero candidate Layer-C organoid outcomes were exposed.** Disposition: organoid-cohort blinding intact with permanent disclosure (`data/exposure_log.jsonl`); no exclusions required; BAGEL file stays quarantined; any future use of those 6 cell lines as development data must cite this log.

## Partitions (corrected)
Layer A = Broad study-specific figshare (inspected; development). Layer C candidates = Sanger outcomes (quarantined; schema-only). **Layer B is NOT carved from inspected minipool outcomes** — B panels are *task definitions for fresh Phase-5 rollouts whose outcomes do not yet exist*; any policy evaluation reusing already-inspected observations is labeled **reused-data engineering evaluation**, not reserved/untouched. Exposure history ships with every split manifest.

## Remaining G1-open (honest)
Donor identities for the 7 minipool models + HCM-SANG↔ACH crosswalk (routes: DepMap Model.csv interactive download; S3 Zenodo registries at Phase 2); genome-wide Broad matrix (interactive portal route); Sanger continuous-matrix identity confirmation via Passports/Miner docs. None blocks the narrowed development estimand in `reviews/g1_question_v2.md`; each gates the specific claims noted there.
# Gate G1 — narrowed identifiable question v2 (for sol-high re-review)

Supersedes v1 candidate. Incorporates G1 review (56/100) + sol-xhigh unstick (`reviews/unstick_g1.md`): six-model **format** analysis, one fixed endpoint, conditional on the selected panel.

## Primary development estimand (identifiable + joinable TODAY)
**Population:** the **223 genes** = 250-gene selected panel − 26 Tier-A selection-tail genes − 1 attrition (`TMOD`), conditional on the fixed selected panel (no genome-wide generalization).
**Environmental measurement (per gene g):** format-bundle lability
`L_g = mean over the 6 format-contrast models of |mean within-model (Chronos_Dome − Chronos_Plastic)|`
(model-equal-weighted; ACH-003040's two pairs averaged first; bundle = Matrigel-dome vs plastic, medium held fixed within pair; Chronos original units).
**Transfer measurement (per gene g, development/descriptive):** `T_g = |MeanDifference_g|` from the published Broad comparison (NextGen organoids n=94 vs lineage-matched adherent n=433; `organoids_vs_2d_gene_dependency_tests_full.csv`).
**Primary parameter:** partial Spearman association ρ(L, T | baseline strength |AdherentMean|) across the 223 genes. One parameter, one contrast, one target family. No gene-class × genotype × context search at G1.
**Join demonstrated:** 223/224 eligible genes join by symbol (attrition recorded). All inputs are manifested Layer-A files.

## Decision rules (disjoint; δ finalized at Lock 1 with this justification)
- Practical-relevance threshold **δ = 0.20** (candidate): rank associations below 0.20 explain <4% of rank variance and would not change target-trust decisions; S3's own transfer signal (ρ≈0.10–0.15) sits below it and is described by its authors as weak. δ is justified by decision relevance, **not** by our exploratory estimate (which is development evidence, already seen: raw ρ=0.167, partial ρ=0.086 — precisely why Broad-side results can never be confirmatory here).
- Development verdicts: `SIGNAL` (CI entirely > δ) / `BOUND` (CI entirely < δ ⇒ informative bound candidate for Lock 2) / `INCONCLUSIVE` (CI straddles δ). CIs from donor/model-cluster joint bootstrap (Phase 2 finalizes; feasibility widths in `data/precision_feasibility_v1.json`: model-bootstrap 95% CI width ≈0.24 around the raw ρ — a bound at δ=0.20 is precision-feasible only if the confirmatory estimate stays low; a SIGNAL claim at δ=0.20 is **not** feasible on current precision and is not promised).

## Confirmatory external endpoint (defined now, gated — Lock 2)
Sanger analogue: frozen per-gene lability values `L_g` (Broad-measured, locked) vs Sanger organoid dependency behavior for the same 223 genes (continuous corrected LFC ranking endpoint, exact form at Lock 1 after: HCM-SANG↔ACH crosswalk, donor-overlap exclusion vs Broad, duplicate-library rule, preprocessing-independence decision — development-only refit from counts or downgraded independence). One locked claim, evaluated once. If the Sanger route cannot be resolved by Lock 1, the external-transfer component is recorded **unmet** and the claim family narrows to the within-Broad environmental bound (still requiring its own independence justification) — recorded scope change per unstick fallback.

## What is explicitly NOT claimed
No causal explanation of transfer failure; no isolated geometry/serum effects; no genome-wide or unselected-gene generalization; no heterogeneity claim from sign flips; no per-gene discoveries at n=6 models (atlas reports continuous effects + uncertainty per gene, claims stay panel-wide).

## Novelty (one sentence, surviving S1/S2/S3)
S1 measured environmental sensitivity without linking it to transfer, and S3 quantified transfer attenuation without environmental attribution; a panel-conditional, selection-controlled test of whether **measured format lability predicts transfer degradation beyond baseline strength** — or a precision-supported bound showing it does not — is unreported in the searched literature (audit: `reviews/novelty_audit_v1.md`, refreshed at G7).

## Recorded scope reductions vs the ambitious goal (honest)
Genome-wide environmental claims: **dropped** (panel-conditional only). Medium-bundle confirmatory claims: deferred (4 models; secondary/descriptive only). Genotype/state-conditional environmental claims: dropped at current support. These components are recorded unmet unless later independent evidence (e.g., additional public controlled-environment data) reopens them under a new study ID.
